# 研究现状总结（2026-10-21，Mo 确认版）

## 定位（不许偏离）
**在 AutoDDG（SIGMOD 2026）既有方法上改进，增强数据检索。** 不改题、不另起炉灶。衡量 = 检索指标（NDCG）+ 元数据质量（忠实性/可读性）。

## 已完成（全部验证过）
- profiler v2：全表事实提取 + 门控（verified/inferred/uncertain/suppressed）+ 七槽位渲染 + UFD/SFD 双通道；修 0 起算 bug、绕开 datamart 截断范围、堵 7 条假事实路径（Codex 反例回归测试全过）
- 实验基建：30 表子集 + 32 NTCIR 查询 + BM25/dense/hybrid 评测 + 配对检验 + 噪声底测量 + 事实审计
- 关键结果：描述忠实性提升（SFD 无支持断言 = 0 vs 原版 27/29 表被污染）；**事实全量注入检索无正收益**（BM25 −0.016、dense +0.003、hybrid 负趋势）
- 诊断：99.9% 词面错配；最大损失 = "事实进 UFD 后 SFD 重新生成丢信息"（−0.012）

## 下一步（三条近期主攻，Codex 提议，按序执行）
1. **程序化锁定 UFD→SFD 保留**（生成环，1-2 人日）：SFD overview 由程序复制 UFD，事实带 ID 清单+覆盖检查+定点修补——直接阻断已定位的最大损失
2. **同预算输入选择**（输入环，2-3 人日）：随机 5 行 → 列覆盖+代表值+少量完整行，三组对比——导师点名的方向
3. **证据约束的查询式扩展**（生成环，2-3 人日）：每表 ≤6 条短检索表达绑定事实 ID，不许新增数据不支持的词——对症词面错配

## Mo 已采纳的新 idea（Codex 第二轮，5 条）
- **#5 辨别式生成（主攻）**：生成 SFD 时喂最易混淆的邻表，描述回答"为什么选我而不是它"——攻击"相关表召回了但排不上去"；先例 speaker-listener，表检索无近邻
- **#1 跨表术语借用（保底）**：用邻表清晰表头/数据字典解释本表模糊缩写——词面错配的新证据源，AutoDDG 没碰过
- **#3 点击失败反哺**：把"打开后返回"归因成粒度/单位/实体问题，决定下一轮 profiling 什么（需要用户研究，排后）
- **#4 主动区分假设**：歧义列让 profiler 挑最能区分解释的操作做（= 之前"自适应 profiler/深度一"的落地版）
- **#6 错误吸引力惩罚**：惩罚"什么都像"的万能描述，抑制 dense hub 表（第一步必须先实测现象是否存在）

## 学术位置（Codex 学术地图，全部联网核实）
- 方法主线 = 可验证保留（方向 1）+ 证据约束查询扩展（方向 2）；忠实性（方向 5）当解释框架
- 负结果的学术价值：对 Doc2Query-- 补充"验证过的真事实也可能无检索价值"；对 Faithful or Findable? 提供"阶段级事实追踪 + 干预证据"
- 必须引用并切割：SANA（Findings ACL 2021，骨架硬约束）、Doc2Query--、AutoDDG、Faithful or Findable?（arXiv 2607.05970）
- 三个引用纠正：全表扫描不是贡献（原版就有）；ECIR 基准来自 Chen et al. ECIR 2020（非 AutoDDG 前作）；"EI"简称存疑（可能是 NTCIR-E1）

# F2 arm (M2): verified facts in the UFD and SFD prompts. Everything else = F1.
# E3: same fact set as F1 (profile_from: F1, identical select); the SFD fact block
# adds no expansion constraint (that is the F2c ablation).
# Facts: profiling_v2.pipeline (datamart + stats + spacetime -> select -> render).
arm: F2
pipeline: autoddg_deep
model: gpt-4o-mini
description_words: 100
description_temperature: 0.0
topic_temperature: 0.0
sfd_temperature: 0.0
sample_size: 100
sample_random_state: 9
semantic_mode: sequential
topic_uses_original_description: true
read_max_rows: null
facts:
  mode: ufd_sfd            # none = bugfix, ufd = F1, ufd_sfd = F2
  profile_from: F1         # reuse F1's profile cache: the very same factset and selection
  select:                    # profiling_v2.select.SelectConfig; defaults, freeze before the test run
    verified_min_rate: 0.95
    inferred_min_rate: 0.80
    uncertain_min_rate: 0.50
    missingness_min_rate: 0.20
    ufd_max_tokens: 160         # one common fact set for F1 and F2; SFD gets its verified/inferred subset
    max_per_kind:
      temporal_span: 3
      temporal_resolution: 3
      spatial_places: 3
      spatial_bbox: 1
      measure_range: 4
      measure_unit: 4
      category_values: 4
      missingness: 3
"""Generate UFD/SFD for one arm with AutoDDG (original package or AutoDDGDeep).

Usage:
    python -I 02_generate.py --arm configs/arm_baseline.yaml --run-id r1 --provider openai   # needs OPENAI_API_KEY
    python -I 02_generate.py --arm configs/arm_bugfix.yaml --stages profile          # offline, no LLM
    python -I 02_generate.py --arm configs/arm_baseline.yaml --dry-run --limit 5     # fake LLM, offline
    python -I 02_generate.py --arm configs/arm_bugfix.yaml --run-id r1 --provider openai \
        --dataset-ids configs/subset30.txt                                           # fixed small subset

Per dataset the call sequence follows examples/quick_start.ipynb:
    sample (100 rows, random_state=9) -> profile_dataframe(full table) -> generate_topic
    -> analyze_semantics(sample, sequential) -> describe_dataset (UFD) -> expand_description_for_search (SFD)

F1 / F2 arms (config key ``facts``): the profile stage also builds the factset
(profiling_v2.pipeline) and renders the UFD / SFD fact blocks; describe_dataset gets
``ufd_facts`` and, with ``facts.mode: ufd_sfd``, expand_description_for_search gets
``sfd_facts``. Everything else is the bug-fix arm. F1 and F2 use one fact set
(E3: the same selected fact ids, F2 only adds the SFD channel): ``facts.profile_from``
points F2 at F1's profile cache, and the profile key covers only what the
profile stage depends on. ``facts.sfd_constraints`` (F2c ablation) adds the
expansion constraints to the SFD fact block.

Caches are bound to the code that produced them (REVIEW_codex_M2 #6):
    profile cache   valid iff table sha256 and ``profile_key`` match; the key
                    hashes the profile-relevant config and ``extractor_version``
                    (AutoDDG git commit, factset schema version, sha256 of the
                    profiler sources, datamart-profiler version)
    generation      valid iff config / prompt hashes, table sha256 and
                    ``fact_input_sha256`` (the fact blocks actually passed to the
                    prompts) match. Caches written before this change have no
                    such keys and are recomputed.

Outputs
    cache/profile/<arm>/<dataset_id>.json          profile stage (no LLM, shared by run ids)
    cache/gen/<arm>/<run_id>/<dataset_id>.json     all stages, prompts, per-call temperatures
    cache/llm/                                     content-addressed LLM responses
    results/descriptions/<arm>__<run_id>.jsonl     one row per corpus dataset

Datasets without a usable table get status "no_table" and no generation; 03_index_eval
indexes title + original description for them in every arm (same degraded input).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import os
import subprocess
import sys
import threading
import time
import traceback
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor, as_completed
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import yaml

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))
from common import (  # noqa: E402
    CACHE_DIR,
    DEFAULT_NTCIR_DIR,
    MANIFEST,
    RESULTS_DIR,
    TABLES_DIR,
    read_jsonl,
    select_benchmark,
    sha256_text,
    write_jsonl,
)
from llm_cache import CachingClient, make_inner_client  # noqa: E402
from tables import load_table  # noqa: E402

import autoddg  # noqa: E402
from autoddg import AutoDDG  # noqa: E402
from autoddg.autoddg_deep import AutoDDGDeep  # noqa: E402
from autoddg.profiling_v2.facts import SCHEMA_VERSION  # noqa: E402
from autoddg.profiling_v2.pipeline import build_factset, fact_texts  # noqa: E402
from autoddg.utils import get_sample  # noqa: E402

LLM_STAGES = ("topic", "semantic", "ufd", "sfd")
AUTODDG_SRC = Path(autoddg.__file__).resolve().parent


def autoddg_commit() -> str:
    try:
        sha = subprocess.run(
            ["git", "-C", str(AUTODDG_SRC), "rev-parse", "HEAD"], capture_output=True, text=True
        ).stdout.strip()
        dirty = subprocess.run(
            ["git", "-C", str(AUTODDG_SRC), "status", "--porcelain", "."],
            capture_output=True,
            text=True,
        ).stdout.strip()
        return sha + ("-dirty" if dirty else "")
    except Exception:
        return "unknown"


def prompt_hash() -> str:
    conf = AUTODDG_SRC / "configurations"
    texts = [(conf / name).read_text(encoding="utf-8") for name in ("prompts.yaml", "prompts_v2.yaml")]
    return sha256_text("\n".join(texts))[:16]


def config_hash(cfg: dict) -> str:
    return sha256_text(json.dumps(cfg, sort_keys=True))[:16]


def fact_mode(cfg: dict) -> str:
    return (cfg.get("facts") or {}).get("mode", "none")


def extractor_sources(cfg: dict) -> list[Path]:
    """Source files the profile stage of this arm runs."""
    dirs = [AUTODDG_SRC / "profiling"]
    if cfg["pipeline"] == "autoddg_deep":
        dirs.append(AUTODDG_SRC / "profiling_v2")
    return sorted(p for d in dirs for p in d.glob("*.py"))


def extractor_version(cfg: dict) -> dict:
    try:
        import datamart_profiler
        dm = getattr(datamart_profiler, "__version__", None)
    except ImportError:
        dm = None
    blob = "\n".join(f"{p.name}\n{p.read_text(encoding='utf-8')}" for p in extractor_sources(cfg))
    return {
        "commit": autoddg_commit(),
        "schema": SCHEMA_VERSION,
        "src_sha256": sha256_text(blob)[:16],
        "datamart_profiler": dm,
    }


def profile_key(cfg: dict) -> str:
    """Hash of everything the profile stage depends on (not the arm name or fact mode)."""
    facts = cfg.get("facts") or {}
    relevant = {
        "pipeline": cfg["pipeline"],
        "read_max_rows": cfg.get("read_max_rows"),
        "facts": fact_mode(cfg) != "none",
        "select": facts.get("select") if fact_mode(cfg) != "none" else None,
        "extractor": extractor_version(cfg),
    }
    return sha256_text(json.dumps(relevant, sort_keys=True))[:16]


def profile_dir(cfg: dict) -> Path:
    return CACHE_DIR / "profile" / ((cfg.get("facts") or {}).get("profile_from") or cfg["arm"])


def profile_cache_valid(cached: dict, table_sha: str, key: str) -> bool:
    return cached.get("table_sha256") == table_sha and cached.get("profile_key") == key


def fact_input_sha(prof: dict, mode: str = "ufd_sfd") -> str:
    """Hash of the fact blocks the prompts actually receive in this fact mode."""
    used = {
        "ufd": prof.get("ufd_facts") if mode != "none" else None,
        "sfd": prof.get("sfd_facts") if mode == "ufd_sfd" else None,
    }
    return sha256_text(json.dumps(used, sort_keys=True))


def gen_cache_valid(cached: dict, meta: dict, table_sha: str, fact_sha: str, want_llm: bool) -> bool:
    return (
        cached.get("config_hash") == meta["config_hash"]
        and cached.get("prompt_hash") == meta["prompt_hash"]
        and cached.get("table_sha256") == table_sha
        and cached.get("fact_input_sha256") == fact_sha
        and cached.get("status") == "ok"
        and (cached.get("ufd") is not None or not want_llm)
    )


def _write_json(path: Path, record: dict) -> None:
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    tmp = path.with_suffix(f".{os.getpid()}.{threading.get_ident()}.tmp")
    tmp.write_text(json.dumps(record, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1), encoding="utf-8")
    tmp.replace(path)  # atomic: F1 and F2 may share a profile file


class _NoClient:
    """Placeholder passed to AutoDDG's constructor; replaced before any call."""


def build_pipe(cfg: dict, client: CachingClient) -> AutoDDG:
    kwargs = {
        "description_temperature": float(cfg["description_temperature"]),
        "description_words": int(cfg["description_words"]),
        "topic_temperature": float(cfg["topic_temperature"]),
    }
    if cfg["pipeline"] == "autoddg":
        if fact_mode(cfg) != "none":
            raise ValueError("the original pipeline has no fact slots")
        if cfg.get("sfd_temperature") is not None:
            raise ValueError("the original pipeline cannot pass an SFD temperature")
        pipe = AutoDDG(_NoClient(), cfg["model"], **kwargs)
    elif cfg["pipeline"] == "autoddg_deep":
        pipe = AutoDDGDeep(
            _NoClient(), cfg["model"], sfd_temperature=float(cfg["sfd_temperature"]),
            fact_mode=fact_mode(cfg),
            sfd_fact_constraints=bool((cfg.get("facts") or {}).get("sfd_constraints", False)),
            **kwargs
        )
    else:
        raise ValueError(f"unknown pipeline {cfg['pipeline']!r}")
    for component in (
        pipe.description_generator,
        pipe.semantic_profiler,
        pipe.topic_generator,
        pipe.search_description,
    ):
        component.llm_client = client
    return pipe


def run_profile(pipe: AutoDDG, cfg: dict, dataset_id: str, table_sha: str, key: str) -> dict:
    out = profile_dir(cfg) / f"{dataset_id}.json"
    if out.exists():
        cached = json.loads(out.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        if profile_cache_valid(cached, table_sha, key):
            return cached
    df, read_info = load_table(TABLES_DIR / f"{dataset_id}.csv", cfg.get("read_max_rows"))
    started = time.time()
    basic_profile, structural_profile = pipe.profile_dataframe(df)
    record = {
        "dataset_id": dataset_id,
        "arm": cfg["arm"],
        "profile_key": key,
        "extractor_version": extractor_version(cfg),
        "table_sha256": table_sha,
        "read": read_info,
        "basic_profile": basic_profile,
        "structural_profile": structural_profile,
        "profile_seconds": round(time.time() - started, 2),
    }
    if fact_mode(cfg) != "none":
        started = time.time()
        fs = build_factset(df, dataset_id, cfg["facts"].get("select"),
                           table={"sha256": table_sha, "read_status": read_info.get("read_status")})
        record.update(fact_texts(fs))
        record["factset"] = fs.to_dict()
        record["facts_seconds"] = round(time.time() - started, 2)
    _write_json(out, record)
    return record


def generate_one(
    entry: dict, manifest_row: dict | None, cfg: dict, args: argparse.Namespace, meta: dict
) -> dict:
    dataset_id = entry["id"]
    base = {"dataset_id": dataset_id, "arm": meta["arm_tag"], "run_id": args.run_id}
    if not manifest_row or manifest_row["status"] not in {"ok", "truncated"}:
        return {**base, "status": "no_table", "download_status": (manifest_row or {}).get("status")}

    out = CACHE_DIR / "gen" / meta["arm_tag"] / args.run_id / f"{dataset_id}.json"
    want_llm = any(s in args.stages for s in LLM_STAGES)
    client = CachingClient(
        make_inner_client(meta["provider"]),
        CACHE_DIR / "llm",
        run_id=args.run_id,
        provider=meta["provider"],
        offline=args.offline,
    )
    record: dict[str, Any] = {
        **base,
        "config": cfg,
        "config_hash": meta["config_hash"],
        "prompt_hash": meta["prompt_hash"],
        "autoddg_commit": meta["autoddg_commit"],
        "provider": meta["provider"],
        "table_sha256": manifest_row["sha256"],
        "download_status": manifest_row["status"],
        "calls": {},
    }
    try:
        pipe = build_pipe(cfg, client)
        # the profile (cached, no LLM) comes first: the generation cache depends on its facts
        prof = run_profile(pipe, cfg, dataset_id, manifest_row["sha256"], meta["profile_key"])
        fact_sha = fact_input_sha(prof, fact_mode(cfg))
        if out.exists():
            cached = json.loads(out.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            if gen_cache_valid(cached, meta, manifest_row["sha256"], fact_sha, want_llm):
                return cached
        fs_budget = (prof.get("factset") or {}).get("budget") or {}
        record.update(read=prof["read"], basic_profile=prof["basic_profile"],
                      structural_profile=prof["structural_profile"], fact_mode=fact_mode(cfg),
                      ufd_facts=prof.get("ufd_facts"), sfd_facts=prof.get("sfd_facts"),
                      profile_key=prof["profile_key"], extractor_version=prof.get("extractor_version"),
                      profile_cache=str(profile_dir(cfg).relative_to(CACHE_DIR.parent) / f"{dataset_id}.json"),
                      fact_input_sha256=fact_sha,
                      selected_ufd=fs_budget.get("selected_ufd"), selected_sfd=fs_budget.get("selected_sfd"))
        if not want_llm:
            record["status"] = "ok"
            return record

        df, _ = load_table(TABLES_DIR / f"{dataset_id}.csv", cfg.get("read_max_rows"))
        sample_df, dataset_sample = get_sample(df, int(cfg["sample_size"]), int(cfg["sample_random_state"]))

        original_description = entry.get("description") if cfg["topic_uses_original_description"] else None
        topic = pipe.generate_topic(entry.get("title") or "", original_description, dataset_sample)
        record["calls"]["topic"] = client.drain_log()

        if cfg["semantic_mode"] != "sequential":
            raise ValueError("only sequential semantic mode is wired")
        semantic_details = pipe.analyze_semantics(sample_df)
        record["calls"]["semantic"] = client.drain_log()
        semantic_profile = "\n".join(
            s for s in [prof["structural_profile"], semantic_details] if s
        )

        ufd_prompt, ufd = pipe.describe_dataset(
            dataset_sample=dataset_sample,
            dataset_profile=prof["basic_profile"],
            use_profile=True,
            semantic_profile=semantic_profile,
            use_semantic_profile=True,
            data_topic=topic,
            use_topic=True,
            **({"ufd_facts": prof.get("ufd_facts")} if fact_mode(cfg) != "none" else {}),
        )
        record["calls"]["ufd"] = client.drain_log()

        sfd_prompt, sfd = pipe.expand_description_for_search(
            ufd, topic, **({"sfd_facts": prof.get("sfd_facts")} if fact_mode(cfg) != "none" else {})
        )
        record["calls"]["sfd"] = client.drain_log()

        sfd_calls = record["calls"]["sfd"]
        record.update(
            status="ok",
            topic=topic,
            semantic_details=semantic_details,
            ufd_prompt_sha256=sha256_text(ufd_prompt),
            sfd_prompt_sha256=sha256_text(sfd_prompt),
            ufd_prompt=ufd_prompt,
            sfd_prompt=sfd_prompt,
            ufd=ufd,
            sfd=sfd,
            sfd_temperature_passed=sfd_calls[-1]["temperature_passed"] if sfd_calls else None,
            sfd_temperature_effective=sfd_calls[-1]["temperature_effective"] if sfd_calls else None,
        )
    except Exception as e:
        record.update(status="error", error=f"{type(e).__name__}: {e}", traceback=traceback.format_exc()[-2000:])
    _write_json(out, record)
    return record


def main() -> None:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawTextHelpFormatter)
    ap.add_argument("--arm", type=Path, required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--run-id", default="r1", help="replicate id; part of the LLM cache key")
    ap.add_argument("--stages", default="profile,topic,semantic,ufd,sfd")
    ap.add_argument("--dry-run", action="store_true", help="fake LLM; writes under <arm>__dryrun")
    ap.add_argument("--offline", action="store_true", help="LLM cache only; fail on miss")
    ap.add_argument(
        "--provider",
        default="none",
        choices=["none", "openai", "fake"],
        help="none = cache only (default; never calls an API); openai needs OPENAI_API_KEY",
    )
    ap.add_argument("--ntcir-dir", type=Path, default=DEFAULT_NTCIR_DIR)
    ap.add_argument("--split", default="test")
    ap.add_argument("--min-relevant", type=int, default=3)
    ap.add_argument("--min-total", type=int, default=1)
    ap.add_argument("--workers", type=int, default=4)
    ap.add_argument("--limit", type=int, default=None)
    ap.add_argument("--ids", default=None, help="comma-separated dataset ids (debug)")
    ap.add_argument("--dataset-ids", type=Path, default=None,
                    help="file with one dataset id per line (e.g. configs/subset30.txt); only these "
                         "are run, output goes to <arm>__<run_id>__<file stem>.jsonl")
    args = ap.parse_args()
    args.stages = tuple(s.strip() for s in args.stages.split(","))

    cfg = yaml.safe_load(args.arm.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    provider = "fake" if args.dry_run else args.provider
    meta = {
        "arm_tag": cfg["arm"] + ("__dryrun" if args.dry_run else ""),
        "provider": provider,
        "config_hash": config_hash(cfg),
        "prompt_hash": prompt_hash(),
        "autoddg_commit": autoddg_commit(),
        "profile_key": profile_key(cfg),
    }
    if any(s in args.stages for s in LLM_STAGES) and provider == "openai" and not args.offline:
        make_inner_client(provider)  # fail fast when the API key is missing

    _, _, corpus = select_benchmark(
        args.ntcir_dir, args.split, args.min_relevant, args.min_total, csv_only=True
    )
    manifest = {r["dataset_id"]: r for r in read_jsonl(MANIFEST)}
    ids = sorted(corpus)
    if args.ids:
        ids = [i for i in args.ids.split(",") if i in corpus]
    if args.dataset_ids:
        listed = [l.strip() for l in args.dataset_ids.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines() if l.strip()]
        missing = [i for i in listed if i not in corpus]
        if missing:
            raise SystemExit(f"{len(missing)} ids in {args.dataset_ids} are not in the corpus: {missing[:3]}")
        ids = [i for i in ids if i in set(listed)]
    if args.limit:
        usable = [i for i in ids if manifest.get(i, {}).get("status") in {"ok", "truncated"}]
        ids = usable[: args.limit]
    print(f"arm={meta['arm_tag']} run={args.run_id} stages={args.stages} datasets={len(ids)} "
          f"autoddg={meta['autoddg_commit']} config={meta['config_hash']} prompts={meta['prompt_hash']} "
          f"profile_key={meta['profile_key']} profile_dir={profile_dir(cfg).name}")

    rows = []
    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=args.workers) as pool:
        futures = {
            pool.submit(generate_one, corpus[i], manifest.get(i), cfg, args, meta): i for i in ids
        }
        for n, fut in enumerate(as_completed(futures), 1):
            r = fut.result()
            rows.append(r)
            extra = r.get("error", "") if r["status"] == "error" else ""
            print(f"[{n}/{len(ids)}] {r['dataset_id']} {r['status']} {extra}", flush=True)

    rows.sort(key=lambda r: r["dataset_id"])
    counts: dict[str, int] = {}
    for r in rows:
        counts[r["status"]] = counts.get(r["status"], 0) + 1
    print(f"status counts: {counts}")
    if not any(s in args.stages for s in LLM_STAGES):
        return  # profile-only runs never overwrite a descriptions file
    keep = ("dataset_id", "arm", "run_id", "status", "download_status", "topic", "ufd", "sfd",
            "sfd_temperature_passed", "sfd_temperature_effective", "fact_mode", "ufd_facts", "sfd_facts",
            "selected_ufd", "selected_sfd", "fact_input_sha256", "profile_key", "config_hash", "prompt_hash",
            "autoddg_commit", "error")
    suffix = f"__{args.dataset_ids.stem}" if args.dataset_ids else ""
    out = RESULTS_DIR / "descriptions" / f"{meta['arm_tag']}__{args.run_id}{suffix}.jsonl"
    write_jsonl(out, [{k: r.get(k) for k in keep} for r in rows])
    print(f"wrote {out}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

"""Fact audit for the F1 / F2 arms: retention, unsupported numbers, budget crowding.

Usage:
    python -I 05_fact_audit.py --arms F1,F2 --run-id r1 --dataset-ids configs/smoke3.txt
    python -I 05_fact_audit.py --arms F1,F2,F2c --run-id r1 --dataset-ids configs/subset30.txt

Reads cache/gen/<arm>/<run_id>/<id>.json and the profile cache it points to
(``profile_cache``), and reports per table and per arm:

retention   for every fact in the common selection, whether the UFD (all
            selected facts) and the SFD (the verified / inferred subset) still
            state it. A fact's anchors are the values its rendered text names
            (span endpoints, places / category values, range bounds, units,
            interval). Writing variants count as kept: "2023-Q1" = "2023 Q1" =
            "Q1 2023", "2016-06-17" = "June 17, 2016", "161.1" = "161" (a
            rounded form of the value). Scalar facts need every anchor; list
            facts (places, categories) need min(2, n) of their shown values.
unsupported years (1800-2100) and other numbers in the UFD / SFD that do not
            occur in that call's own prompt (sample, profile, facts, topic,
            UFD for the SFD): the generator had no input for them.
crowding    promptable facts left out of the common selection by the token
            budget or a per-kind cap, with their kinds and priorities.
identity    with several arms: per table, whether topic, semantic notes, UFD
            prompt, UFD text and selected fact ids are identical (E3 requires
            this for F1 vs F2).

Output: results/audit/<arms>__<run_id>[__<stem>].json and a summary on stdout.
Retention measures literal restatement only; whether a restated fact is used
correctly still needs the manual check of the selected facts.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import re
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import pandas as pd

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))
from common import CACHE_DIR, HERE, RESULTS_DIR  # noqa: E402

LIST_KINDS = ("spatial_places", "category_values")
MAX_LIST_ANCHORS = 5  # render.UFD_TOP_VALUES
INTERVAL_WORDS = {
    "year": ["year", "annual"], "half-year": ["half year", "semiannual", "semi annual"],
    "quarter": ["quarter"], "month": ["month"], "week": ["week"], "day": ["day", "daily"],
    "hour": ["hour"], "minute": ["minute"],
}
_NUM = re.compile(r"(?<![\w.])-?\d+(?:\.\d+)?(?![\w])")
_YEAR = re.compile(r"(?<!\d)(1[89]\d\d|20\d\d|2100)(?!\d)")


# --- normalisation ---------------------------------------------------------------

def norm(text: str) -> str:
    """Lower case; thousands commas dropped; - _ / , ; : ( ) as spaces; one space."""
    t = str(text).lower()
    t = re.sub(r"(?<=\d),(?=\d{3}\b)", "", t)
    t = re.sub(r"[-_/,;:()–—]+", " ", t)
    return re.sub(r"\s+", " ", t).strip()


def _contains(text_n: str, phrase: str) -> bool:
    p = norm(phrase)
    return bool(p) and re.search(rf"(?<![a-z0-9]){re.escape(p)}(?![a-z0-9])", text_n) is not None


def time_variants(value: str) -> list[str]:
    """Writings of a span endpoint that count as the same value."""
    v = str(value)
    out = [v]
    m = re.fullmatch(r"(\d{4})-Q([1-4])", v)
    if m:
        y, q = m.groups()
        ordinal = ["first", "second", "third", "fourth"][int(q) - 1]
        return out + [f"{y} Q{q}", f"Q{q} {y}", f"{ordinal} quarter of {y}"]
    if re.fullmatch(r"\d{4}", v):
        return out
    try:
        ts = pd.Timestamp(v)
    except (ValueError, TypeError):
        return out
    month, mon = ts.strftime("%B"), ts.strftime("%b")
    if re.fullmatch(r"\d{4}-\d{2}", v):
        return out + [f"{month} {ts.year}", f"{mon} {ts.year}"]
    day = f"{ts.day}"
    out += [f"{month} {day} {ts.year}", f"{mon} {day} {ts.year}", f"{day} {month} {ts.year}",
            f"{ts.month}/{ts.day}/{ts.year}"]
    return out


def numbers_in(text: str) -> list[str]:
    t = re.sub(r"(?<=\d),(?=\d{3}\b)", "", str(text))
    return _NUM.findall(t)


def number_matches(anchor: float, found: str) -> bool:
    """``found`` is ``anchor`` or a rounded writing of it."""
    x = float(found)
    decimals = len(found.split(".")[1]) if "." in found else 0
    if decimals == 0 and not float(anchor).is_integer() and abs(anchor) < 10:
        return False  # "2" is too loose a writing of 1.81
    return abs(x - anchor) <= 0.5 * 10 ** -decimals + 1e-9


def has_number(text: str, anchor: float) -> bool:
    return any(number_matches(anchor, n) for n in numbers_in(text))


# --- anchors ---------------------------------------------------------------------

def anchors(fact: dict) -> list[dict]:
    """[{"kind": "text"|"number", "alts" | "value"}] the fact's rendered text names."""
    k, v = fact["kind"], fact["value"]
    out: list[dict] = []

    def text(*alts):
        out.append({"kind": "text", "alts": [a for a in alts if a]})

    def num(x):
        if x is not None:
            out.append({"kind": "number", "value": float(x)})

    if k == "temporal_span":
        for end in (v["start"], v["end"]):
            text(*time_variants(end))
    elif k == "temporal_resolution" and v.get("basis") == "observed_interval":
        text(*INTERVAL_WORDS.get(v.get("interval"), [v.get("interval")]))
    elif k == "spatial_places":
        if v.get("confirmed"):
            for p in v["places"][:MAX_LIST_ANCHORS]:
                text(p["value"])
        else:
            text(v["level"])
    elif k == "spatial_level":
        text(v["level"])
    elif k == "category_values":
        for t in v["top"][:MAX_LIST_ANCHORS]:
            text(t["value"])
    elif k == "measure_range":
        if v.get("trimmed"):
            num(v.get("trimmed_min")), num(v.get("trimmed_max"))
        elif v.get("mixed_units"):
            for g in v["by_unit"]:
                num(g["min"]), num(g["max"])
        else:
            num(v.get("min")), num(v.get("max"))
    elif k == "measure_unit":
        if v.get("basis") == "unit_column":
            for u in v["units"][:MAX_LIST_ANCHORS]:
                text(u["value"])
        elif v.get("unit") == "mixed":
            for u in v.get("units", []):
                text(u["value"])
        else:
            text(v["unit"])
    elif k == "table_size":
        num(v.get("n_rows"))
    elif k == "missingness":
        num(round(100 * v["null_rate"]))
    return out


def anchor_found(a: dict, text: str, text_n: str) -> bool:
    if a["kind"] == "number":
        return has_number(text, a["value"])
    for alt in a["alts"]:
        alt = str(alt)
        if alt and not alt[0].isalnum() and alt in text:  # "$", "%"
            return True
        if _contains(text_n, alt):
            return True
    return False


def fact_kept(fact: dict, text: str) -> tuple[bool, int, int]:
    """(kept, anchors found, anchors)."""
    anc = anchors(fact)
    if not anc:
        return True, 0, 0
    text_n = norm(text)
    hits = sum(anchor_found(a, text, text_n) for a in anc)
    need = min(2, len(anc)) if fact["kind"] in LIST_KINDS else len(anc)
    return hits >= need, hits, len(anc)


# --- unsupported numbers -----------------------------------------------------------

def unsupported(output: str, prompt: str) -> dict:
    """Years and other numbers in ``output`` that do not occur in ``prompt``."""
    prompt_nums = numbers_in(prompt)
    years = sorted(set(_YEAR.findall(output)))
    bad_years = [y for y in years if y not in prompt_nums]
    others = sorted({n for n in numbers_in(output) if n not in years and len(n.lstrip("-").replace(".", "")) >= 2})
    bad_nums = [n for n in others if not any(number_matches(float(p), n) or p == n for p in prompt_nums)]
    return {"n_years": len(years), "unsupported_years": bad_years,
            "n_numbers": len(others), "unsupported_numbers": bad_nums}


# --- per table -----------------------------------------------------------------------

def load_gen(arm: str, run_id: str, dataset_id: str) -> dict | None:
    p = CACHE_DIR / "gen" / arm / run_id / f"{dataset_id}.json"
    if not p.exists():
        return None
    rec = json.loads(p.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    return rec if rec.get("status") == "ok" and rec.get("ufd") is not None else None


def load_factset(rec: dict) -> dict | None:
    rel = rec.get("profile_cache")
    if not rel:
        return None
    prof = json.loads((HERE / rel).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    if prof.get("profile_key") != rec.get("profile_key"):
        raise SystemExit(f"{rec['dataset_id']}: profile cache changed since generation; regenerate first")
    return prof.get("factset")


def crowding(fs: dict) -> dict:
    by_id = {f["fact_id"]: f for f in fs["facts"]}
    b = fs.get("budget") or {}
    out = {"used_tokens": b.get("used_ufd_tokens"), "max_tokens": b.get("ufd_max_tokens"),
           "n_selected": len(b.get("selected_ufd", [])), "dropped": []}
    for d in b.get("dropped", []):
        if d["reason"] in ("budget", "kind_cap"):
            f = by_id[d["id"]]
            out["dropped"].append({"id": d["id"], "reason": d["reason"], "kind": f["kind"],
                                   "priority": f["priority"], "status": f["status"]})
    out["n_dropped"] = len(out["dropped"])
    n_cand = out["n_selected"] + out["n_dropped"]
    out["crowded_share"] = out["n_dropped"] / n_cand if n_cand else 0.0
    return out


def audit_table(rec: dict, fs: dict) -> dict:
    by_id = {f["fact_id"]: f for f in fs["facts"]}
    b = fs.get("budget") or {}
    res = {"dataset_id": rec["dataset_id"], "arm": rec["arm"]}
    for target in ("ufd", "sfd"):
        ids = b.get(f"selected_{target}", [])
        rows = []
        for i in ids:
            kept, hits, n = fact_kept(by_id[i], rec[target])
            rows.append({"id": i, "kind": by_id[i]["kind"], "kept": kept, "anchors_found": hits, "anchors": n,
                         "text": (by_id[i].get("render") or {}).get("ufd")})
        res[f"{target}_retention"] = {
            "n_facts": len(rows),
            "n_kept": sum(r["kept"] for r in rows),
            "rate": sum(r["kept"] for r in rows) / len(rows) if rows else None,
            "missed": [r for r in rows if not r["kept"]],
        }
        res[f"{target}_unsupported"] = unsupported(rec[target], rec.get(f"{target}_prompt") or "")
    res["crowding"] = crowding(fs)
    res["unresolved"] = fs.get("unresolved", [])
    return res


IDENTITY_KEYS = ("topic", "semantic_details", "ufd_facts", "ufd_prompt", "ufd", "selected_ufd")


def identity(recs: dict[str, dict]) -> dict:
    """Per arm pair: the keys whose values differ from the first arm's."""
    arms = list(recs)
    ref = recs[arms[0]]
    return {f"{arms[0]}=={arm}": [k for k in IDENTITY_KEYS if recs[arm].get(k) != ref.get(k)]
            for arm in arms[1:]}


def _summary(rows: list[dict]) -> dict:
    def pooled(key):
        n = sum(r[key]["n_facts"] for r in rows)
        k = sum(r[key]["n_kept"] for r in rows)
        return {"n_facts": n, "n_kept": k, "rate": k / n if n else None}

    def unsup(key):
        ny = sum(r[key]["n_years"] for r in rows)
        nn = sum(r[key]["n_numbers"] for r in rows)
        by = sum(len(r[key]["unsupported_years"]) for r in rows)
        bn = sum(len(r[key]["unsupported_numbers"]) for r in rows)
        return {"years": ny, "unsupported_years": by, "numbers": nn, "unsupported_numbers": bn,
                "rate": (by + bn) / (ny + nn) if ny + nn else None}

    sel = sum(r["crowding"]["n_selected"] for r in rows)
    drop = sum(r["crowding"]["n_dropped"] for r in rows)
    return {
        "n_tables": len(rows),
        "ufd_retention": pooled("ufd_retention"),
        "sfd_retention": pooled("sfd_retention"),
        "ufd_unsupported": unsup("ufd_unsupported"),
        "sfd_unsupported": unsup("sfd_unsupported"),
        "crowding": {"n_selected": sel, "n_dropped": drop,
                     "share": drop / (sel + drop) if sel + drop else None,
                     "tables_with_drops": sum(r["crowding"]["n_dropped"] > 0 for r in rows)},
    }


def _fmt_rate(x) -> str:
    return "-" if x is None else f"{x:.0%}"


def main() -> None:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawTextHelpFormatter)
    ap.add_argument("--arms", default="F1,F2")
    ap.add_argument("--run-id", default="r1")
    ap.add_argument("--dataset-ids", type=Path, required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--out", type=Path, default=None)
    args = ap.parse_args()
    arms = [a.strip() for a in args.arms.split(",") if a.strip()]
    ids = [l.strip() for l in args.dataset_ids.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines() if l.strip()]

    per_arm: dict[str, list[dict]] = {a: [] for a in arms}
    ident, missing = {}, []
    for i in ids:
        recs = {}
        for arm in arms:
            rec = load_gen(arm, args.run_id, i)
            fs = load_factset(rec) if rec else None
            if rec is None or fs is None:
                missing.append(f"{arm}/{i}")
                continue
            recs[arm] = rec
            per_arm[arm].append(audit_table(rec, fs))
        if len(recs) > 1:
            ident[i] = identity(recs)

    report = {"arms": arms, "run_id": args.run_id, "dataset_ids": str(args.dataset_ids),
              "missing": missing, "summary": {a: _summary(r) for a, r in per_arm.items() if r},
              "identity": ident, "tables": per_arm}
    stem = f"__{args.dataset_ids.stem}"
    out = args.out or RESULTS_DIR / "audit" / f"{'_'.join(arms)}__{args.run_id}{stem}.json"
    out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    out.write_text(json.dumps(report, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1), encoding="utf-8")

    for arm, s in report["summary"].items():
        u, f = s["ufd_unsupported"], s["sfd_unsupported"]
        print(f"[{arm}] tables={s['n_tables']}  "
              f"UFD kept {s['ufd_retention']['n_kept']}/{s['ufd_retention']['n_facts']} "
              f"({_fmt_rate(s['ufd_retention']['rate'])})  "
              f"SFD kept {s['sfd_retention']['n_kept']}/{s['sfd_retention']['n_facts']} "
              f"({_fmt_rate(s['sfd_retention']['rate'])})")
        print(f"      unsupported UFD years {u['unsupported_years']}/{u['years']} numbers "
              f"{u['unsupported_numbers']}/{u['numbers']}; SFD years {f['unsupported_years']}/{f['years']} "
              f"numbers {f['unsupported_numbers']}/{f['numbers']}")
        c = s["crowding"]
        print(f"      crowding: {c['n_dropped']} of {c['n_selected'] + c['n_dropped']} promptable facts "
              f"left out ({_fmt_rate(c['share'])}), {c['tables_with_drops']} tables")
        for r in per_arm[arm]:
            miss = [f"{m['id']}:{m['kind']}" for m in r["sfd_retention"]["missed"]]
            print(f"      {r['dataset_id'][:8]} UFD {r['ufd_retention']['n_kept']}/{r['ufd_retention']['n_facts']} "
                  f"SFD {r['sfd_retention']['n_kept']}/{r['sfd_retention']['n_facts']} "
                  f"SFD missed {miss} unsupported UFD {r['ufd_unsupported']['unsupported_years'] + r['ufd_unsupported']['unsupported_numbers']} "
                  f"SFD {r['sfd_unsupported']['unsupported_years'] + r['sfd_unsupported']['unsupported_numbers']}")
    for i, d in ident.items():
        bad = {k: v for k, v in d.items() if v}
        print(f"identity {i[:8]}: {'identical' if not bad else bad}")
    if missing:
        print(f"missing (no ok generation or no profile): {missing}")
    print(f"wrote {out}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

scripts; the library code lives in `external/AutoDDG` (own git repo, branch
`autoddg-deep` on top of 3c59e1c; the original package files are untouched).

## Environment

```bash
/opt/homebrew/bin/python3.11 -m venv .venvs/autoddg-deep
.venvs/autoddg-deep/bin/pip install -c experiments/autoddg_deep/constraints_autoddg_3c59e1c.txt \
    -e external/AutoDDG desbordante==2.5.0 datamart-geo pytest
```

The shell profile sets `PYTHONPATH` to a py3.14 site-packages, which breaks the
venv. Run every script with `python -I` (ignores `PYTHONPATH`).

## Evaluation set

`data_search_2_e_test` topics with >= 3 relevant judgments = 32 queries
(`--min-relevant 3 --min-total 1`, the default everywhere). The corpus is the
judged pool of those queries restricted to datasets with a CSV file (483).

## Pipeline

```bash
PY=../../.venvs/autoddg-deep/bin/python
$PY -I 00_fetch_tables.py --split test --csv-only --judged-only      # data/tables, data/manifest.jsonl
$PY -I 02_generate.py --arm configs/arm_baseline.yaml --stages profile   # offline profile stage
$PY -I 02_generate.py --arm configs/arm_baseline.yaml --run-id r1 --provider openai
$PY -I 02_generate.py --arm configs/arm_baseline.yaml --run-id r2 --provider openai   # noise floor
$PY -I 02_generate.py --arm configs/arm_bugfix.yaml   --run-id r1 --provider openai
$PY -I 03_index_eval.py --arms baseline__r1,baseline__r2,bugfix__r1,human --tag m1
$PY -I 04_paired_test.py --eval results/eval/m1.json --a baseline__r1 --b bugfix__r1 --noise-a2 baseline__r2
```

`--provider` defaults to `none` (LLM cache only, never calls an API).
`--dry-run` runs everything with a fake LLM under `<arm>__dryrun`.

Tests: `$PY -I -m pytest tests` here and `$PY -I -m pytest tests` in `external/AutoDDG`.

## Smoke run (2026-10-21)

`OPENAI_API_KEY` is read by `openai.OpenAI()` in `llm_cache.make_inner_client`;
non-interactive shells need `source ~/.zshrc` first. Both arms, `--run-id smoke --limit 5`:
4/5 ok per arm. `01dca46b` (598 columns) fails at the UFD call with
`context_length_exceeded` (gpt-4o-mini, 128k) in both arms. 11 tables have >= 200
columns and 7 tables are unreadable by `load_table`; how to treat them in the full
run is still open.

Full run (baseline x2 noise floor + bugfix):

```bash
source ~/.zshrc
cd experiments/autoddg_deep
PY=../../.venvs/autoddg-deep/bin/python
$PY -I 02_generate.py --arm configs/arm_baseline.yaml --run-id r1 --provider openai 2>&1 | tee logs/gen_baseline_r1.log
$PY -I 02_generate.py --arm configs/arm_baseline.yaml --run-id r2 --provider openai 2>&1 | tee logs/gen_baseline_r2.log
$PY -I 02_generate.py --arm configs/arm_bugfix.yaml   --run-id r1 --provider openai 2>&1 | tee logs/gen_bugfix_r1.log
```

Re-running the same command resumes: finished datasets come from `cache/gen`,
LLM calls from `cache/llm`.

## M2: fact arms F1 / F2

`configs/arm_F1.yaml` = bugfix + verified facts in the UFD prompt (`facts.mode: ufd`);
`configs/arm_F2.yaml` = F1 + the same facts in the SFD prompt (`facts.mode: ufd_sfd`).
The two configs differ only in `facts.mode` (and `facts.profile_from: F1`, so F2 reads
F1's profile cache); F1 differs from bugfix only by the `facts` block.
E3 (M2c): one selection serves both arms. `selected_ufd` is the common fact set,
`selected_sfd` its verified / inferred subset; the SFD fact block adds no expansion
constraint. `configs/arm_F2c.yaml` (`facts.sfd_constraints: true`) is the separate
ablation that adds "keep verbatim, add synonyms, do not extend"; compare it with F2 only.

The profile stage builds the factset with `autoddg.profiling_v2.pipeline.build_factset`
(datamart + stats + spacetime -> `select` -> `render` -> budget) and stores it in
`cache/profile/<arm>/<id>.json` together with the rendered `ufd_facts` / `sfd_facts`.
Status thresholds, the token budget and per-kind caps are under `facts.select`
(defaults, to be frozen before the test run). UFD gets verified / inferred / uncertain
facts ("likely" prefix), SFD only verified / inferred. The full UFD / SFD prompts are
kept in `cache/gen/<arm>/<run>/<id>.json`.

Cache validity (M2c): a profile cache entry is reused only if its `profile_key` matches
(AutoDDG commit, factset schema, sha256 of the profiler sources, datamart-profiler
version, profile-relevant config); a generation entry only if `fact_input_sha256` (the
fact blocks the prompts actually got) matches too. Entries written before M2c have
neither key and are recomputed; LLM calls with unchanged prompts still come from
`cache/llm`.

Fact audit (no LLM): `$PY -I 05_fact_audit.py --arms F1,F2 --run-id r1 --dataset-ids configs/smoke3.txt`
reports UFD / SFD retention of the selected facts (writing variants such as
"2023-Q1" / "2023 Q1" count as kept), years and numbers not found in the call's own
prompt, facts crowded out by the budget, and whether F1 and F2 share topic, semantic
notes, UFD prompt, UFD text and selected fact ids. Output: `results/audit/`.

Smoke run (3 tables in `configs/smoke3.txt`, run id `r1` so topic and semantic calls are
cache hits from the bugfix subset30 run):

```bash
$PY -I 02_generate.py --arm configs/arm_F1.yaml --run-id r1 --provider openai --dataset-ids configs/smoke3.txt
$PY -I 02_generate.py --arm configs/arm_F2.yaml --run-id r1 --provider openai --dataset-ids configs/smoke3.txt
```
    d = b - a
    if d.size == 0:
        return {"n_queries": 0}
    wins = int(np.sum(d > TIE_EPS))
    losses = int(np.sum(d < -TIE_EPS))
    lo, hi = bootstrap_ci(d, args.bootstrap, rng)
    return {
        "n_queries": int(d.size),
        "mean_a": float(a.mean()),
        "mean_b": float(b.mean()),
        "delta": float(d.mean()),
        "wins": wins,
        "ties": int(d.size - wins - losses),
        "losses": losses,
        "p_randomization": randomization_test(d, args.n_perm, rng),
        "ci95_low": lo,
        "ci95_high": hi,
        "per_query_delta": dict(zip(common, map(float, d))),
    }


def main() -> None:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawTextHelpFormatter)
    ap.add_argument("--eval", type=Path, required=True, help="output of 03_index_eval.py")
    ap.add_argument("--a", required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--b", required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--noise-a2", default=None, help="second run of arm A for the noise floor")
    ap.add_argument("--metrics", default="ndcg@10,ndcg@20")
    ap.add_argument("--test", default="randomization", choices=["randomization"])
    ap.add_argument("--n-perm", type=int, default=10000)
    ap.add_argument("--bootstrap", type=int, default=10000)
    ap.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=9)
    args = ap.parse_args()

    per_query = json.loads(args.eval.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))["per_query"]

    def settings_of(arm: str) -> dict[str, dict]:
        out = {}
        for key, scores in per_query.items():
            name, view, *rest = key.split("|")
            if name == arm:
                out["|".join([view, *rest])] = scores
        return out

    A, B = settings_of(args.a), settings_of(args.b)
    A2 = settings_of(args.noise_a2) if args.noise_a2 else {}
    if args.a == "human" or args.b == "human":
        # the human arm has a single view; compare it against every view of the other arm
        if args.a == "human":
            A = {k: next(iter(A.values())) for k in B} if A else {}
        else:
            B = {k: next(iter(B.values())) for k in A} if B else {}
    shared = sorted(set(A) & set(B))
    if not shared:
        raise SystemExit(f"no shared settings for {args.a} and {args.b} in {args.eval}")

    rows = []
    for setting in shared:
        for metric in args.metrics.split(","):
            rng = np.random.default_rng(args.seed)
            res = compare(A[setting], B[setting], metric, args, rng)
            if res["n_queries"] == 0:
                continue
            noise = None
            if setting in A2:
                noise_res = compare(A[setting], A2[setting], metric, args, np.random.default_rng(args.seed))
                noise = abs(noise_res.get("delta", 0.0))
            direction_ok = res["wins"] > res["losses"] if res["delta"] > 0 else res["losses"] > res["wins"]
            res.update(
                setting=setting,
                metric=metric,
                noise_floor=noise,
                effective=(None if noise is None else bool(abs(res["delta"]) > noise and direction_ok)),
            )
            rows.append(res)
            print(
                f"{setting:42s} {metric:8s} n={res['n_queries']:2d} "
                f"A={res['mean_a']:.4f} B={res['mean_b']:.4f} d={res['delta']:+.4f} "
                f"W/T/L={res['wins']}/{res['ties']}/{res['losses']} p={res['p_randomization']:.4f} "
                f"CI=[{res['ci95_low']:+.4f},{res['ci95_high']:+.4f}]"
                + (f" noise={noise:.4f} effective={res['effective']}" if noise is not None else "")
            )

    out_dir = RESULTS_DIR / "paired"
    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    stem = f"{args.a}__vs__{args.b}"
    meta = {"eval": str(args.eval), "a": args.a, "b": args.b, "noise_a2": args.noise_a2,
            "n_perm": args.n_perm, "bootstrap": args.bootstrap, "seed": args.seed}
    (out_dir / f"{stem}.json").write_text(json.dumps({"meta": meta, "rows": rows}, indent=1), encoding="utf-8")
    fields = ["setting", "metric", "n_queries", "mean_a", "mean_b", "delta", "wins", "ties", "losses",
              "p_randomization", "ci95_low", "ci95_high", "noise_floor", "effective"]
    with open(out_dir / f"{stem}.csv", "w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        w = csv.DictWriter(f, fieldnames=fields, extrasaction="ignore")
        w.writeheader()
        w.writerows(rows)
    print(f"wrote {out_dir / (stem + '.json')}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from beartype import beartype

from ..llm import LLMClient
from ..utils import load_prompts


@beartype
class SearchFocusedDescription:
    """Create search-optimised dataset descriptions

    Expand a human-readable description into a search-optimised outline
    """

    def __init__(self, client: LLMClient | Any, model_name: str = "gpt-4o-mini") -> None:
        # Support both LLMClient and legacy OpenAI clients
        if isinstance(client, LLMClient):
            self.llm_client = client
        else:
            # Legacy support: wrap OpenAI-compatible client
            from ..llm import OpenAICompatibleClient

            self.llm_client = OpenAICompatibleClient(client)
        self.model = model_name
        prompts = load_prompts()["search_expansion"]
        self._template = prompts["template"].strip()
        self._system_message = prompts["system_message"].strip()
        self._user_prompt = prompts["user_prompt"]

    def expand_description(self, initial_description: str, topic: str) -> tuple[str, str]:
        """Expand an initial dataset description for retrieval tasks

        Args:
            initial_description: Baseline description that will be enriched
            topic: Topic context anchoring the search-optimised output

        Returns:
            (prompt, expanded_description)
        """

        prompt = self._user_prompt.format(
            topic=topic,
            initial_description=initial_description,
            template=self._template,
        )

        response = self.llm_client.chat_completions_create(
            model=self.model,
            messages=[
                {"role": "system", "content": self._system_message},
                {"role": "user", "content": prompt},
            ],
        )

        expanded_description = response["choices"][0]["message"]["content"]
        return prompt, expanded_description
"""LLM client wrappers for the experiment scripts.

CachingClient sits between AutoDDG and the real client:
  * every request is content-addressed (model, messages, temperature as passed,
    kwargs, run_id) and stored under cache/llm/, so re-runs read from disk and a
    second run_id gives an independent replicate for the noise floor;
  * the temperature each call actually received is recorded, including
    "not passed" (None) for the original SemanticProfiler/SFD calls, whose
    effective value is the client default.

FakeClient returns schema-valid canned answers so the whole pipeline can be
exercised offline (--dry-run). Its outputs are never written next to real runs.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import os
import re
import threading
import time
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from autoddg.llm import LLMClient, OpenAICompatibleClient

NOT_PASSED = None  # recorded value when the caller did not pass a temperature
OPENAI_DEFAULT_TEMPERATURE = 0.0  # autoddg.llm.openai_compat default


class FakeClient(LLMClient):
    """Offline stand-in that answers each AutoDDG prompt type with valid output."""

    COLUMN = {
        "Domain-Specific Types": "General",
        "Entity Type": "Entity",
        "Function/Usage Context": "Measurement",
        "Spatial": {"isSpatial": False, "resolution": ""},
        "Temporal": {"isTemporal": False, "resolution": ""},
    }

    def chat_completions_create(self, model, messages, temperature=0.0, **kwargs):
        user = messages[-1]["content"]
        if "Columns to analyze" in user:
            names = re.findall(r"^Column: (.+)$", user, re.M)
            content = json.dumps({n: self.COLUMN for n in names})
        elif "semantic analyzer" in user:
            content = json.dumps(self.COLUMN)
        elif "Topic (2-3 words)" in user:
            content = "Dry Run Topic"
        elif "expand the description" in user:
            content = "Dataset Overview: dry run search description. Keywords: dry, run."
        else:
            digest = hashlib.sha256(user.encode()).hexdigest()[:8]
            content = f"Dry run description {digest}."
        return {"choices": [{"message": {"role": "assistant", "content": content}}], "usage": None}


class CachingClient(LLMClient):
    def __init__(
        self,
        inner: LLMClient | None,
        cache_dir: Path,
        run_id: str,
        provider: str,
        offline: bool = False,
    ):
        self.inner = inner
        self.provider = provider
        # one directory per provider so fake (dry-run) answers can never be served to a real run
        self.cache_dir = Path(cache_dir) / provider
        self.cache_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        self.run_id = run_id
        self.offline = offline  # cache-only: raise on miss instead of calling the API
        self.log: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        self._lock = threading.Lock()

    def _key(self, model, messages, temperature, kwargs) -> str:
        payload = {
            "model": model,
            "messages": messages,
            "temperature": temperature,
            "kwargs": kwargs,
            "run_id": self.run_id,
            "provider": self.provider,
        }
        blob = json.dumps(payload, sort_keys=True, ensure_ascii=False)
        return hashlib.sha256(blob.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()

    def chat_completions_create(self, model, messages, temperature=NOT_PASSED, **kwargs):
        key = self._key(model, messages, temperature, kwargs)
        path = self.cache_dir / key[:2] / f"{key}.json"
        hit = path.exists()
        if hit:
            record = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        else:
            if self.offline or self.inner is None:
                raise RuntimeError(f"LLM cache miss with no live client ({key[:12]})")
            call_kwargs = dict(kwargs)
            if temperature is not NOT_PASSED:
                call_kwargs["temperature"] = temperature
            started = time.time()
            response = self.inner.chat_completions_create(model=model, messages=messages, **call_kwargs)
            record = {
                "key": key,
                "model": model,
                "temperature_passed": temperature,
                "temperature_effective": (
                    OPENAI_DEFAULT_TEMPERATURE if temperature is NOT_PASSED else temperature
                ),
                "kwargs": kwargs,
                "run_id": self.run_id,
                "provider": self.provider,
                "messages": messages,
                "response": response,
                "latency_s": round(time.time() - started, 3),
                "created_at": time.strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S"),
            }
            path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            tmp = path.with_suffix(".tmp")
            tmp.write_text(json.dumps(record, ensure_ascii=False), encoding="utf-8")
            tmp.replace(path)
        with self._lock:
            self.log.append(
                {
                    "key": key,
                    "cache_hit": hit,
                    "model": model,
                    "temperature_passed": record["temperature_passed"],
                    "temperature_effective": record["temperature_effective"],
                    "usage": record["response"].get("usage"),
                }
            )
        return record["response"]

    def drain_log(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        with self._lock:
            out, self.log = self.log, []
        return out


def make_inner_client(provider: str) -> LLMClient | None:
    if provider == "none":
        return None  # cache-only; any miss raises
    if provider == "fake":
        return FakeClient()
    if provider == "openai":
        if not os.environ.get("OPENAI_API_KEY"):
            raise SystemExit(

  template: |
    Dataset Overview:
    - Please keep the exact initial description of the dataset as shown in beginning the prompt.

    Key Themes or Topics:
    - Central focus on a broad area of interest (e.g., urban planning, socio-economic factors, environmental analysis).
    - Data spans multiple subtopics or related areas that contribute to a holistic understanding of the primary theme.
    Example:
    - theme1/topic1
    - theme2/topic2
    - theme3/topic3

    Applications and Use Cases:
    - Facilitates analysis for professionals, policymakers, researchers, or stakeholders.
    - Useful for specific applications, such as planning, engineering, policy formulation, or statistical modeling.
    - Enables insights into patterns, trends, and relationships relevant to the domain.
    Example:
    - application1/usecase1
    - application2/usecase2
    - application3/usecase3

    Concepts and Synonyms:
    - Includes related concepts, terms, and variations to ensure comprehensive coverage of the topic.
    - Synonyms and alternative phrases improve searchability and retrieval effectiveness.
    Example:
    - concept1/synonym1
    - concept2/synonym2
    - concept3/synonym3

    Keywords and Themes:
    - Lists relevant keywords and themes for indexing, categorization, and enhancing discoverability.
    - Keywords reflect the dataset's content, scope, and relevance to the domain.
    Example:
    - keyword1
    - keyword2
    - keyword3

    Additional Context:
    - Highlights the dataset's relevance to specific challenges or questions in the domain.
    - May emphasize its value for interdisciplinary applications or integration with related datasets.
    Example:
    - context1
    - context2
    - context3
  user_prompt: |
    You are given a dataset about the topic {topic}, with the following initial description:

    {initial_description}

    Please expand the description by including the exact topic. Additionally, add as many related concepts, synonyms, and relevant terms as possible based on the initial description and the topic.

    Unlike the initial description, which is focused on presentation and readability, the expanded description is intended to be indexed at the backend of a dataset search engine to improve searchability.

    Therefore, focus less on readability and more on including all relevant terms related to the topic. Make sure to include any variations of the key terms and concepts that could help improve retrieval in search results.

    Please follow the structure of the following example template:
    {template}

# M2 fact slots (IMPL §1.3). Both are inserted into the original prompts by
# the subclasses in description/generator_factaware.py and

dataset_description:
  # inserted right before the original closing_instruction. Hard-fact
  # boundary (REVIEW_codex_M2 suggestion 6): no new coverage / values /
  # units / granularity, a missing fact is unknown rather than absent, and
  # topic links, likely uses and synonyms stay allowed as long as they are
  # not presented as facts about the table.
  coverage_instruction: |
    The following facts were computed from the full table. A qualifier inside a fact (such as "of 80% parseable values", "excluding 3 values", "of 70 non-blank") is part of that fact; facts marked "likely" or "probably" are less certain, so keep that word. If facts are listed for them, state the observation unit and the spatial and temporal coverage in the first sentence, using these exact names and years. Do not add places or years not listed, and do not add other coverage, numeric values, units or granularity that are neither listed below nor visible in the data. A property not listed below is unknown, not absent: do not say the table lacks it. You may still relate the dataset to its topic, likely uses, related concepts and synonyms, as long as these are not presented as facts about the table's contents.
    {ufd_facts}

search_expansion:
  # E3 channel only (REVIEW_codex_M2 #7): F2 adds the facts and a section to
  # hold them, and no expansion constraint beyond the parent prompt's, so
  # that F1 -> F2 changes the channel alone.
  # inserted into the original template right after the Dataset Overview section
  verified_section: |
    Verified Coverage and Measures:
    - The facts given above, as written.
  # inserted into the original user_prompt right before its template instruction
  facts_instruction: |
    The following facts were computed from the full table:

    {verified_facts}

    Include them in the "Verified Coverage and Measures" section.
  # ablation arm F2c only (facts.sfd_constraints: true), appended after facts_instruction
  constraint_instruction: |
    Keep these facts exactly as written and add common variants and synonyms of the same fact (e.g. annual/yearly, county-level/by county); do not change or extend the years, places or values.
    pools = args.pools.split(",")
    subsets = args.subsets.split(",")
    tag = args.tag or "__".join(arms)

    queries, qrels, corpus = select_benchmark(
        args.ntcir_dir, args.split, args.min_relevant, args.min_total, csv_only=args.corpus == "csv"
    )
    manifest = {r["dataset_id"]: r for r in read_jsonl(MANIFEST)}
    with_table = {d for d in corpus if manifest.get(d, {}).get("status") in {"ok", "truncated"}}
    listed: set[str] = set()
    if args.dataset_ids:
        listed = {l.strip() for l in args.dataset_ids.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines() if l.strip()}
        if "listed" not in subsets:
            subsets.append("listed")
    qids = sorted(queries)
    print(f"queries={len(qids)} corpus={len(corpus)} with_table={len(with_table)} corpus_mode={args.corpus}")

    encoder = DenseEncoder() if any(r in {"dense", "hybrid"} for r in retrievers) else None
    qvecs = dict(zip(qids, encoder.queries([queries[q] for q in qids]))) if encoder else {}

    per_query: dict[str, dict] = {}
    coverage: dict[str, dict] = {}
    for arm in arms:
        for view in (["title+description"] if arm == "human" else args.views.split(",")):
            docs, cov = arm_documents(arm, view, corpus)
            name = f"{arm}|{view}"
            coverage[name] = cov
            dvecs = {}
            if encoder:
                ids = sorted(docs)
                dvecs = dict(zip(ids, encoder.passages([docs[d] for d in ids])))
            for pool in pools:
                for subset in subsets:
                    allowed = {"with_table": with_table, "listed": listed & set(corpus)}.get(subset, set(corpus))
                    global_ids = sorted(allowed)
                    for retriever in retrievers:
                        key = f"{name}|{retriever}|{pool}|{subset}"
                        scores = {}
                        for q in qids:
                            judgments = {d: r for d, r in qrels[q].items() if d in allowed}
                            if not any(r > 0 for r in judgments.values()):
                                continue  # no relevant dataset left in this pool
                            cand = sorted(judgments) if pool == "per_query" else global_ids
                            ranked = {}
                            if retriever in {"bm25", "hybrid"}:
                                ranked["bm25"] = rank_bm25(queries[q], cand, docs)
                            if retriever in {"dense", "hybrid"}:
                                ranked["dense"] = rank_dense(qvecs[q], cand, dvecs)
                            final = rrf(ranked["bm25"], ranked["dense"]) if retriever == "hybrid" else ranked[retriever]
                            scores[q] = {f"ndcg@{k}": ndcg_at_k(final, judgments, k) for k in ks}
                        per_query[key] = scores
                        means = {m: float(np.mean([s[m] for s in scores.values()])) for m in (f"ndcg@{k}" for k in ks)}
                        print(f"{key:70s} n_q={len(scores):2d} " + " ".join(f"{m}={v:.4f}" for m, v in means.items()))

    out_dir = RESULTS_DIR / "eval"
    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    settings = {
        "split": args.split, "min_relevant": args.min_relevant, "min_total": args.min_total,
        "corpus": args.corpus, "n_queries": len(qids), "n_corpus": len(corpus),
        "n_with_table": len(with_table), "dataset_ids": str(args.dataset_ids) if args.dataset_ids else None,
        "n_listed": len(listed), "bm25": {"k1": BM25_K1, "b": BM25_B, "tokenizer": "lower().split()"},

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from beartype import beartype

from ..llm import LLMClient
from ..profiling_v2.prompts import load_prompts_v2
from .search import SearchFocusedDescription

TEMPLATE_ANCHOR = "\n\nKey Themes or Topics:"
PROMPT_ANCHOR = "Please follow the structure of the following example template:"


@beartype
class FactAwareSearchDescription(SearchFocusedDescription):
    """Search-focused description with a verified-facts slot and explicit temperature

    The parent never passes a temperature, so the value comes from the
    client default (0.0 for OpenAICompatibleClient, greedy for LocalLLMClient).
    This subclass passes it explicitly and keeps it on ``last_call`` so the
    experiment scripts can write it next to the output.

    With ``verified_facts`` (rendered SFD slots: verified / inferred facts
    only), the template gets a "Verified Coverage and Measures" section after
    "Dataset Overview" and the user prompt gets the facts right before its
    template instruction. Without facts the prompt is the parent's prompt.

    The fact channel adds no expansion constraint of its own (E3 changes the
    channel only). ``fact_constraints=True`` appends
    ``constraint_instruction`` (keep verbatim, add synonyms, do not extend)
    after the facts; it is the separate F2c ablation.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        client: LLMClient | Any,
        model_name: str = "gpt-4o-mini",
        temperature: float = 0.0,
        fact_constraints: bool = False,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(client, model_name)
        self.temperature = float(temperature)
        self.fact_constraints = bool(fact_constraints)
        self.last_call: dict[str, Any] = {}
        v2 = load_prompts_v2()["search_expansion"]
        if TEMPLATE_ANCHOR not in self._template or PROMPT_ANCHOR not in self._user_prompt:
            raise RuntimeError("search_expansion prompts changed; fact slot anchors not found")
        self._fact_template = self._template.replace(
            TEMPLATE_ANCHOR, "\n\n" + v2["verified_section"].strip() + TEMPLATE_ANCHOR, 1
        )
        facts_block = v2["facts_instruction"]
        if self.fact_constraints:
            facts_block += v2["constraint_instruction"]
        self._fact_user_prompt = self._user_prompt.replace(
            PROMPT_ANCHOR, facts_block + "\n" + PROMPT_ANCHOR, 1
        )

    def build_prompt(self, initial_description: str, topic: str,
                     verified_facts: str | None = None) -> str:
        if not verified_facts:
            return self._user_prompt.format(
                topic=topic,
                initial_description=initial_description,
                template=self._template,
            )
        return self._fact_user_prompt.format(
            topic=topic,
            initial_description=initial_description,
            verified_facts=verified_facts,
            template=self._fact_template,
        )

    def expand_description(
        self,
        initial_description: str,
        topic: str,
        verified_facts: str | None = None,
        temperature: float | None = None,
    ) -> tuple[str, str]:
        """Expand an initial dataset description for retrieval tasks

        Args:
            initial_description: Baseline description that will be enriched
            topic: Topic context anchoring the search-optimised output
            verified_facts: Rendered SFD fact slots; None or "" = parent prompt
            temperature: Overrides the instance temperature for this call

        Returns:
            (prompt, expanded_description)
        """

        temp = self.temperature if temperature is None else float(temperature)
        prompt = self.build_prompt(initial_description, topic, verified_facts)

        response = self.llm_client.chat_completions_create(
            model=self.model,
            messages=[
                {"role": "system", "content": self._system_message},
                {"role": "user", "content": prompt},
            ],
            temperature=temp,
        )

        self.last_call = {"model": self.model, "temperature": temp,
                          "verified_facts": bool(verified_facts)}
        if self.fact_constraints:
            self.last_call["fact_constraints"] = True
        expanded_description = response["choices"][0]["message"]["content"]
        return prompt, expanded_description
**Dataset Overview:**
This dataset focuses on driving under the influence, providing insights into the prevalence of DUI incidents across five states: Iowa, Kansas, Missouri, Nebraska, and the broader United States. It includes data for the years 2012 and 2014, with values ranging from 1.8 to 3.4 for 2012 and 1.3 to 2.8 for 2014. The dataset captures spatial information through state-level locations and coordinates, allowing for geographic analysis. Researchers and policymakers can utilize this dataset to understand trends in DUI incidents over time and develop targeted interventions to improve road safety.

**Verified Coverage and Measures:**
- Identity: 5 rows, 4 columns
- Geographic scope: State (state) includes Iowa, Kansas, Missouri, United States, Nebraska (state-level, by state)
- Measures and units: 2012: 1.8 to 3.4 (median 2.1); 2014: 1.3 to 2.8 (median 1.7)
- Representative categories: Location: Iowa (42.469401411000035, -93.81648725599996) (20%), Kansas (38.347742794000055, -98.20077822999997), Missouri (38.63579147100006, -92.56629944499997), United States (39.759995942000046, -98.49999896099996), Nebraska (41.64104362100005, -99.36571888999998)

\"\"\"Immutable UFD overview with bounded, cached LLM supplements and local repair.\"\"\"
import re
from .search_factaware import FactAwareSearchDescription
from .fact_coverage import fact_kept


def words(text):
    return len(text.split())


def prefix(text, budget):
    matches = list(re.finditer(r'\\S+', text))
    return text[:matches[budget-1].end()] if 0 < budget < len(matches) else (text if budget > 0 else '')


class LockedSearchDescription(FactAwareSearchDescription):
    def generate(self, ufd, topic, verified_facts, budget):
        overview = 'Dataset Overview:\\n' + ufd
        remaining = budget - words(overview)
        if remaining < 1:
            raise ValueError('F2 budget cannot hold immutable overview plus supplements')
        template = self._template.split('Key Themes or Topics:', 1)[1]
        prompt = self._user_prompt.format(topic=topic, initial_description=ufd,
                    template='Key Themes or Topics:' + template)
        prompt += ('\\nFull-table facts (same input as F2):\\n' + (verified_facts or '') +
                   '\\nThe Dataset Overview is copied verbatim by the program. Generate ONLY the '
                   'remaining themes, applications, concepts, keywords and additional context sections. '
                   f'Do not generate an overview. Use at most {remaining} whitespace-separated words.')
        response = self.llm_client.chat_completions_create(model=self.model,
            messages=[{'role':'system','content':self._system_message}, {'role':'user','content':prompt}],
            temperature=self.temperature)
        supplement = response['choices'][0]['message']['content']
        return prompt, overview + '\\n\\n' + prefix(supplement, remaining)

    @staticmethod
    def repair(ufd, sfd, facts, budget):
        \"\"\"Monotone repairs; trimming supplements can expose further missing facts.\"\"\"
        overview = 'Dataset Overview:\\n' + ufd
        if not sfd.startswith(overview + '\\n\\n'):
            raise ValueError('Overview was modified')
        supplement = sfd[len(overview)+2:]
        repaired = []
        current = sfd
        while True:
            missing = [f for f in facts if not fact_kept(f, current)[0]]
            if not missing:
                return current, repaired
            for f in missing:
                if f['fact_id'] in [r['id'] for r in repaired]:
                    raise ValueError('Rendered fact fails coverage check: ' + f['fact_id'])
                text = f['render']['ufd']
                if not text:
                    raise ValueError('Missing fact has no rendered evidence')
                repaired.append({'id':f['fact_id'], 'text':text})
            patch = '\\n\\nVerified Coverage and Measures:\\n' + '\\n'.join('- '+r['text'] for r in repaired)
            remaining = budget - words(overview + patch)
            if remaining < 0:
                raise ValueError('Immutable overview and repairs exceed F2 budget')
            current = overview + patch + '\\n\\n' + prefix(supplement, remaining)

\"\"\"SFD-only intervention; fail closed on missing F2 caches or non-subset30 inputs.\"\"\"
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path
import yaml
sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))
from common import CACHE_DIR, HERE, RESULTS_DIR, write_jsonl, sha256_text
from llm_cache import CachingClient, make_inner_client
from autoddg.description.search_locked import LockedSearchDescription, words
import autoddg.description.search_locked as implementation
import autoddg.description.fact_coverage as coverage


def main():
    ap=argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    ap.add_argument('--arm', type=Path, required=True)
    ap.add_argument('--run-id', default='r1')
    ap.add_argument('--offline', action='store_true')
    args=ap.parse_args()
    cfg=yaml.safe_load(args.arm.read_text()); lock=cfg['locked_sfd']
    ref=yaml.safe_load((HERE/'configs/arm_F2.yaml').read_text())
    assert {k:v for k,v in cfg.items() if k not in ('arm','locked_sfd')} == {k:v for k,v in ref.items() if k"'!='"'arm'}
    ids=(HERE/'configs/subset30.txt').read_text().split()
    assert len(ids)==len(set(ids))==30
    rows=[]
    source_hash=sha256_text(Path(implementation.__file__).read_text()+Path(coverage.__file__).read_text())
    for i in ids:
        path=CACHE_DIR/'gen'/lock['source_arm']/lock['source_run']/f'{i}.json'
        source=json.loads(path.read_text()); assert source['status']=='ok'
        prof_path=HERE/source['profile_cache']; prof=json.loads(prof_path.read_text())
        assert prof['profile_key']==source['profile_key'] and prof['table_sha256']==source['table_sha256']
        assert prof['factset']['budget']['selected_sfd']==source['selected_sfd']
        fingerprint=sha256_text(path.read_text()+prof_path.read_text()+json.dumps(cfg,sort_keys=True)+source_hash)
        out=CACHE_DIR/'gen'/cfg['arm']/args.run_id/f'{i}.json'
        if out.exists() and (cached:=json.loads(out.read_text())).get('locked_fingerprint')==fingerprint:
            rows.append(cached); continue
        client=CachingClient(None if args.offline else make_inner_client('openai'), CACHE_DIR/'llm',
                             args.run_id, 'openai', offline=args.offline)
        engine=LockedSearchDescription(client,cfg['model'],cfg['sfd_temperature'])
        budget=words(source['sfd'])
        prompt,sfd=engine.generate(source['ufd'],source['topic'],source['sfd_facts'],budget)
        original=sfd; repairs=[]
        if lock['coverage_repair']:
            by_id={f['fact_id']:f for f in prof['factset']['facts']}
            sfd,repairs=engine.repair(source['ufd'],sfd,[by_id[k] for k in source['selected_sfd']],budget)
        record={**source,'arm':cfg['arm'],'run_id':args.run_id,'config':cfg,
                'config_hash':sha256_text(json.dumps(cfg,sort_keys=True)),
                'sfd':sfd,'sfd_prompt':prompt,'sfd_prompt_sha256':sha256_text(prompt),
                'calls':{'sfd':client.drain_log()}, 'locked_fingerprint':fingerprint,
                'locked_source':str(path.relative_to(HERE)), 'locked_source_sha256':sha256_text(path.read_text()),
                'locked_code_sha256':source_hash,'locked_budget_words':budget,
                'locked_pre_repair':original,'locked_repairs':repairs}
        assert words(sfd)<=budget
        out.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True); out.write_text(json.dumps(record,ensure_ascii=False,indent=1))
        rows.append(record)
        print(f'{cfg[\"arm\"]} {i} words={words(sfd)}/{budget} repairs={len(repairs)}',flush=True)
    write_jsonl(RESULTS_DIR/'descriptions'/f'{cfg[\"arm\"]}__{args.run_id}__subset30.jsonl',rows)
    print(f'Completed {len(rows)} tables')

if __name__=='__main__': main()

import pytest
from autoddg.description.search_locked import LockedSearchDescription, words
from autoddg.description.fact_coverage import fact_kept


def fact(i, value):
    return {'fact_id':i,'kind':'temporal_span','value':{'start':value,'end':value},
            'render':{'ufd':'Coverage: '+value}}


def test_normalized_quarter():
    assert fact_kept(fact('F1','2023-Q1'),'2023 Q1')[0]


def test_repair_trim_cascade_and_immutable_overview():
    ufd='Verbatim  spacing\\nand newline.'
    sfd='Dataset Overview:\\n'+ufd+'\\n\\n'+'keywords '*20+'2020'
    fixed, repairs=LockedSearchDescription.repair(ufd,sfd,[fact('F1','2021'),fact('F2','2020')],words(sfd))
    assert fixed.startswith('Dataset Overview:\\n'+ufd+'\\n\\n')
    assert len(repairs)==2
    assert all(fact_kept(fact('F',y),fixed)[0] for y in ('2020','2021'))
    assert words(fixed)<=words(sfd)
    again, repairs2=LockedSearchDescription.repair(ufd,fixed,[fact('F1','2021')],words(sfd))
    assert again==fixed and repairs2==[]


def test_impossible_budget_fails():
    with pytest.raises(ValueError,match='exceed'):
        LockedSearchDescription.repair('text','Dataset Overview:\\ntext\\n\\nmore',[fact('F1','2021')],3)
