"""Index each arm's descriptions and score NDCG@k per query.

Usage:
    python -I 03_index_eval.py --arms baseline__r1,bugfix__r1 --k 10,20 --retrievers bm25,dense,hybrid
    python -I 03_index_eval.py --arms human --tag sanity          # title + original NTCIR description

An arm is a file results/descriptions/<arm>.jsonl from 02_generate.py, or the
built-in "human" arm (title + original description, no LLM). For every arm the
document text of a dataset is

    title + <view>        view in {ufd, sfd}, when 02_generate produced it
    title + description   otherwise (no table / generation error): the same
                          degraded input in every arm

Settings, identical across arms:
  bm25    rank_bm25.BM25Okapi, k1=1.5 b=0.75, tokens = text.lower().split()
          (as autoddg.ranking.metrics.downstream_task_rank)
  dense   fastembed BAAI/bge-small-en-v1.5, cosine (query_embed / passage_embed)
  hybrid  reciprocal rank fusion of bm25 and dense, k_rrf=60
  NDCG    gain 2^rel - 1, log2 discount, ideal = the query's judgments within the pool
  pool    per_query: rank only the query's judged datasets (AutoDDG NTCIR protocol)
          global:    rank the whole corpus
  subset  all | with_table (queries re-scored on datasets that have a usable table)
          | listed (queries re-scored on the ids in --dataset-ids; added automatically)

Writes results/eval/<tag>.json (per-query scores) and results/eval/<tag>_summary.csv.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import csv
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
from rank_bm25 import BM25Okapi

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))
from common import (  # noqa: E402
    CACHE_DIR,
    DEFAULT_NTCIR_DIR,
    MANIFEST,
    RESULTS_DIR,
    read_jsonl,
    select_benchmark,
    sha256_text,
)

DENSE_MODEL = "BAAI/bge-small-en-v1.5"
RRF_K = 60
BM25_K1, BM25_B = 1.5, 0.75


def tokenize(text: str) -> list[str]:
    return text.lower().split()


def ndcg_at_k(ranked_ids: list[str], judgments: dict[str, int], k: int) -> float:
    gains = [2 ** judgments.get(d, 0) - 1 for d in ranked_ids[:k]]
    dcg = sum(g / np.log2(i + 2) for i, g in enumerate(gains))
    ideal = sorted((2**r - 1 for r in judgments.values()), reverse=True)[:k]
    idcg = sum(g / np.log2(i + 2) for i, g in enumerate(ideal))
    return float(dcg / idcg) if idcg > 0 else float("nan")


class DenseEncoder:
    """fastembed encoder with a per-text on-disk cache (same vectors for every arm)."""

    def __init__(self, model_name: str = DENSE_MODEL):
        from fastembed import TextEmbedding

        self.model_name = model_name
        self.model = TextEmbedding(model_name)
        self.cache_path = CACHE_DIR / "emb" / f"{model_name.replace('/', '__')}.npz"
        self.cache: dict[str, np.ndarray] = {}
        if self.cache_path.exists():
            with np.load(self.cache_path) as z:
                self.cache = {k: z[k] for k in z.files}

    def _encode(self, texts: list[str], kind: str) -> np.ndarray:
        keys = [f"{kind}_{sha256_text(t)}" for t in texts]
        missing = [(k, t) for k, t in zip(keys, texts) if k not in self.cache]
        if missing:
            embed = self.model.query_embed if kind == "q" else self.model.passage_embed
            for (k, _), v in zip(missing, embed([t for _, t in missing])):
                self.cache[k] = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float32)
            self.cache_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            np.savez(self.cache_path, **self.cache)
        m = np.stack([self.cache[k] for k in keys])
        return m / np.linalg.norm(m, axis=1, keepdims=True)

    def queries(self, texts: list[str]) -> np.ndarray:
        return self._encode(texts, "q")

    def passages(self, texts: list[str]) -> np.ndarray:
        return self._encode(texts, "p")

\"\"\"Assemble reproducible Chinese report; unavailable runs remain explicitly pending.\"\"\"
import json
from pathlib import Path
B=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
R=B/'results'
complete=(R/'eval/l1l2.json').exists()
eval_path=R/'eval'/('l1l2.json' if complete else 'l1l2_reference.json')
ev=json.loads(eval_path.read_text())
noise=json.loads((R/'paired/baseline__r1__subset30__vs__baseline__r2__subset30.json').read_text())['rows']
audit=json.loads((R/'audit'/('F2_L1_L2__r1__subset30.json' if complete else 'F2__r1__subset30.json')).read_text())
lines=['# L1/L2：程序化锁定 UFD→SFD（subset30）','',
'状态：'+('实验完成。' if complete else '**实现完成，实验未完成。** 首个 L1 API 请求在域名解析阶段失败（httpcore.ConnectError，Errno 8），未取得新响应；L1/L2 指标与 p 值不得用旧结果代填。'),'',
'## 实现与实验边界','',
'- AutoDDG 仓库仅新增 description/search_locked.py、fact_coverage.py；原包既有文件不改。实验仓库的 05_fact_audit.py 导入抽出的同一归一/覆盖逻辑。',
'- L1：Dataset Overview 逐字复制 F2 缓存中的 UFD，LLM 仅输出主题、应用、概念、关键词与上下文补充。事实输入不变。',
'- L2：与 L1 复用同一请求缓存，按 selected_sfd 的 ID 检查，遗漏事实原渲染文本追加到 Verified Coverage and Measures；不调用修补 LLM。删除补充尾部腾出预算后再检查，直至全部覆盖或明确报错。',
'- 一个缓存事实 F027 将 3.9998 渲染为 4，原审计拒绝该小数近似；追加同一 fact.value 的精确端点，不放宽审计。',
'- 上游直接读取 F2/r1 和其所指向的 F1 profile，不调用 profiling/topic/semantic/UFD，也不因新 commit 使旧缓存失效。指纹绑定源记录、profile、配置和实现。',
'- 固定 subset30：29 张有成功的 F2 SFD；fbb92a2a-4b64-4ac8-ba0f-8d608bfc47ec 原 F2 因 212272 输入 token 超过 128000 而失败，新臂继承相同失败和 title+description 回退。',
'- F2 SFD 原本无显式输出上限。新臂每表采用对应 F2 SFD 的 whitespace 词数上限（含标题），复制与修补均计入；这是同上限控制，不宣称实际词数严格相等。若必须严格等长，应另立协议后重跑，不能用无证据填充凑字数。',
'- 因新臂取消了 LLM 的 overview/事实专段生成，并增加预算指令，L2−F2 是所实现机制的总干预效果，不能严称为仅“重新生成丢信息”的无混杂因果估计。L2−L1 使用完全相同的补充文本，能隔离覆盖修补及其预算挤占效应。',
'- 评测沿用 03_index_eval.py：BM25 k1=1.5、b=0.75、lower().split()，sfd/per_query，NDCG@10/20；配对随机化 10000 次、bootstrap 10000 次、seed=9。all 是主结果，with_table/listed 是敏感性结果。32 个原查询中 CSV 判断池有正相关的为 25 个，with_table 为 18 个，listed 为 8 个。仅对 30 张生成；all 评测保留既有全判断池，其余文档统一原描述回退。','',
'## 噪声底：baseline r2 − r1','',
'|池|指标|n|r1|r2|Δ|p|95% CI|','|---|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---|']
for r in noise:
 lines.append(f\"|{r['setting'].split('|')[-1]}|{r['metric']}|{r['n_queries']}|{r['mean_a']:.6f}|{r['mean_b']:.6f}|{r['delta']:+.6f}|{r['p_randomization']:.4f}|[{r['ci95_low']:+.6f}, {r['ci95_high']:+.6f}]|\")
lines+=['','噪声底只是一对重复的经验差值，不是可靠的显著性阈值；超过它仍需看配对 p 值、区间和方向一致性。','', '## 四组对照','', 'Δ 均为目标臂减参照臂；L1 vs L2 按 L2−L1 报告。']
for a,b in [('bugfix','L2'),('F2','L2'),('F2','L1'),('L1','L2')]:
 lines+=['',f'### {b} − {a}','']
 if not complete:
  lines+=['待运行：均值、逐查询差值、p 值及置信区间尚不可计算。']
  continue
 rs=json.loads((R/f'paired/{a}__r1__subset30__vs__{b}__r1__subset30.json').read_text())['rows']
 lines+=['|池|指标|Δ|p|95% CI|胜/平/负|噪声底绝对值|','|---|---|---:|---:|---|---|---:|']
 for r in rs:
  n=next(n for n in noise if (n['setting'],n['metric'])==(r['setting'],r['metric']))
  lines.append(f\"|{r['setting'].split('|')[-1]}|{r['metric']}|{r['delta']:+.6f}|{r['p_randomization']:.4f}|[{r['ci95_low']:+.6f},{r['ci95_high']:+.6f}]|{r['wins']}/{r['ties']}/{r['losses']}|{abs(n['delta']):.6f}|\")
 for subset in ('all','with_table','listed'):
  lines+=['',f'逐查询（{subset}）：','', '|query|Δ@10|Δ@20|噪声Δ@10|噪声Δ@20|','|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|']
  rr=[next(r for r in rs if r['setting'].endswith('|'+subset) and r['metric']==m) for m in ('ndcg@10','ndcg@20')]
  nn=[next(r for r in noise if r['setting'].endswith('|'+subset) and r['metric']==m) for m in ('ndcg@10','ndcg@20')]
  for q in rr[0]['per_query_delta']:
   vals=[r['per_query_delta'][q] for r in rr+nn]
   lines.append('|'+q+'|'+'|'.join(f'{v:+.6f}' for v in vals)+'|')
lines+=['','## 事实保留率','', '|臂|成功表|SFD 保留|保留率|','|---|---:|---|---:|']
for a in ('F2','L1','L2'):
 s=audit['summary'].get(a)
 if s:
  f=s['sfd_retention'];lines.append(f\"|{a}|{s['n_tables']}|{f['n_kept']}/{f['n_facts']}|{f['rate']:.4%}|\")
 else: lines.append(f'|{a}|待运行|待运行|待运行|')
lines+=['','该口径是旧审计的锚点保留：列表只要求 min(2,n) 个值，不等于全部列表值、列值关系或语义正确性得到证明。L2 的精确端点来自程序证据，若旧 unsupported-number 检查只看 LLM prompt，可能将其标成未支持，需结合 locked_repairs 和原 fact.value 核对。','',
'## 结论与续跑','',
'程序结构保证 overview 不经 LLM 重写；成功返回的 L2 保证 selected_sfd 通过现有覆盖谓词。检索是否改善、覆盖检查值多少，必须等真实 L1/L2 生成和配对结果，当前不能下结论。','',
'执行 "'`zsh experiments/autoddg_deep/run_l1l2.sh`：source ~/.zshrc（不显示 key），L1 在线、L2 缓存模式，随后评测/配对/审计并重建本报告。脚本遇错立即停止；不会扩大生成表集。缓存及 results/ 默认被 gitignore 忽略，报告与脚本纳入版本控制。'"']
(B/'REPORT_L1L2.md').write_text('\\n'.join(lines)+'\\n')
print('wrote REPORT_L1L2.md; complete=',complete)

def test_generate_exact_length_and_verbatim_overview():
    from autoddg.llm import LLMClient
    class Stub(LLMClient):
        def chat_completions_create(self, model, messages, temperature=0.0, **kwargs):
            assert 'full-table facts' in messages[-1]['content']
            return {'choices':[{'message':{'content':'Themes: '+ 'context '*100}}]}
    engine=LockedSearchDescription(Stub())
    ufd='Keep  whitespace\\nand punctuation.'
    _, result=engine.generate(ufd,'topic','full-table facts',40)
    assert result.startswith('Dataset Overview:\\n'+ufd+'\\n\\n')
    assert words(result)==40


def test_short_generation_fails_instead_of_padding():
    from autoddg.llm import LLMClient
    class Stub(LLMClient):
        def chat_completions_create(self, **kwargs):
            return {'choices':[{'message':{'content':'short'}}]}
    with pytest.raises(ValueError,match='shorter'):
        LockedSearchDescription(Stub()).generate('ufd','topic','facts',40)
